### 2026-10-24 05:22:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Canada’s 10-Year Yield Edges Higher on BoC Rate Hike Bets — Canada’s 10-year government bond yield edged higher to around 3.85% in October as expectations for Bank of Canada policy shifted toward tighter monetary conditions. Markets anticipate a more hawkish tone at the BoC’s next policy decision, scheduled for October 28. Governor Tiff Macklem recently said the central bank expects to significantly raise its quarterly inflation forecast this month, following hawkish remarks last week about energy-driven price pressures. Rising oil prices have added to upward pressure on energy costs amid the escalation of the conflict in the Middle East. Meanwhile, Canadian retail sales are expected to have increased 0.7% month over month in September, extending a 1.2% gain in August, according to preliminary estimates. Stronger retail activity could add to inflation concerns and reinforce expectations for a tighter monetary policy stance.

### 2026-10-24 05:17:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Paraguay #Producer Prices Change #Importance 0

Paraguay Producer Prices Fall for Eighth Consecutive Month — Paraguay’s Producer Price Index fell 0.8% year-on-year in September 2026, marking its eighth consecutive annual decrease. The decline was mainly driven by negative contributions from domestic agriculture and horticultural products and live animals and animal products, as well as imported metallic products, machinery and equipment, and food, beverages and tobacco, textiles, and leather products. Meanwhile, domestic non-metallic transportable goods and other manufactured products, along with imported non-metallic transportable goods, made positive contributions, helping to mitigate the overall decline. By product grouping, both domestic and imported products contributed negatively to the annual result.

### 2026-10-24 05:06:52  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 1

Ibovespa Closes Higher Ahead of Presidential Runoff — The Ibovespa rose 0.5% to close at 205,083 on Friday in the final session before Brazil’s presidential runoff, recovering from earlier losses. A Datafolha poll showed President Lula and Flávio Bolsonaro tied at 50% each of valid votes. Earlier polls indicated a statistical tie. Some market participants view a potential Flávio Bolsonaro administration as more likely to rein in public spending, which they consider essential to lowering interest rates. However, neither the candidate nor his economic advisers have outlined concrete fiscal adjustments. The Ibovespa rallied to record highs after Flávio Bolsonaro finished ahead of Lula in the first round. Meanwhile, mid-month inflation came in below expectations, supporting equities. Banking stocks advanced, with BB gaining 3.3%. Bradesco added 0.7% following third-quarter earnings in line with market expectations and the announcement of a share buyback program. Petrobras rose 1.7% as oil prices rose. For the week, the Ibovespa fell 1.0%.

### 2026-10-24 04:36:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Paraguay #Interest Rate #Importance 0

Paraguay Central Bank Holds Policy Rate at 5.50% — Paraguay’s central bank kept its policy rate unchanged at 5.50% at it's October 2026 meeting. The decision came as economic activity remained strong, with the central bank revising its 2026 GDP growth forecast upward from 4.5% to 5.0%, supported by services and utilities. Annual inflation stood at 1.7% in September, while core inflation excluding food and energy reached 1.5%. The central bank lowered its year-end inflation forecast from 3.3% to 3.0%, reflecting a more moderate path for non-energy goods prices, and cut its core inflation projection from 2.8% to 2.2%. Inflation expectations remained anchored at 3.5%. Meanwhile, external risks persisted amid elevated oil prices and geopolitical tensions in the Middle East, alongside expectations of higher US interest rates through the remainder of 2026 and into 2027. The central bank reaffirmed its commitment to price stability and its 3.5% inflation target.

### 2026-10-24 04:34:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Uruguay #Unemployment Rate #Importance 0

Uruguay Unemployment Rate Edged Lower in September — Uruguay’s unemployment rate inched down to 7.1% in September 2026 from 7.2% in August. By region, unemployment was equal in Montevideo and the interior, at 7.1%. The gender gap persisted, with female unemployment at 8.6%, compared with 5.8% for men. Unemployment varied significantly by age, reaching 21.6% among young people aged 14 to 24, the group facing the greatest difficulties entering the labor market. Regarding employment quality, underemployment stood at 8.8%, while the share of workers not registered with social security reached 21%. Both indicators remained broadly stable month-over-month.

【China: IAEA should stop reviewing Iran nuclear issue】China’s deputy UN representative Sun Lei told the General Assembly on the 23rd that UN Security Council resolution 2231 has expired and that the Security Council and the IAEA should cease reviewing Iran’s nuclear file; the IAEA director‑general should stop submitting related reports. Sun warned US‑UK‑Australia nuclear‑powered submarine cooperation contravenes the NPT’s purpose, poses serious proliferation risks and challenges the treaty and IAEA safeguards. Iran-allied Houthi militants have continued attacks on Saudi Arabia, prompting the US State Department to order non-essential personnel to leave the country. ### 2026-10-24 04:18:21  #國際

【中方：國際原子能機構應停止審議伊核問題】中國常駐聯合國副代表孫磊23日在聯大審議“國際原子能機構年度報告”時發言，指出國際原子能機構應停止審議伊朗核問題。孫磊說，國際原子能機構應秉持客觀公正立場，嚴格根據授權履行職責，為有關各方相向而行、恢復對話談判提供協助。伊核問題最終要透過外交途徑妥善解決，訴諸武力、制裁施壓只會激化矛盾。伊朗作為《不擴散核武器條約》無核武器締約國，應履行不發展核武器承諾。伊朗和平利用核能的合法權利應得到尊重。他說，核可伊朗核問題全面協議的安理會第2231號決議已到期終止，安理會及國際原子能機構應停止審議伊核問題，國際原子能機構總幹事應停止提交有關報告。孫磊指出，美英澳核潛艇合作違反《不擴散核武器條約》宗旨和目標，存在嚴重核擴散風險，對條約和國際原子能機構保障監督體系構成嚴峻挑戰。國際社會已充分認識到有關問題的複雜性與敏感性，應在平等和相互尊重基礎上，透過公開、透明、包容、可持續的政府間程序，尋求各方均可接受的解決方案。（新華社）

Escalating Middle East tensions pushed oil higher, stoking inflation concerns ahead of the Federal Reserve’s interest rate decision. Treasury yields rose after the market’s strongest session in over a year. Financial stocks dipped, with JPMorgan down 1.5%, Morgan Stanley falling 2.4% and BlackRock losing 1.7%. American Express dropped 3.7% despite beating earnings expectations and announcing plans to cut 5% of its global workforce. Among AI hyperscalers, Alphabet lost 1.2%, Meta shed 1% and Amazon retreated 0.9%. Concerns over rising debt to finance AI infrastructure pressured tech stocks this week. Most chipmakers recovered on Friday but still posted sharp weekly losses. For the week, the S&P 500 fell 1%, the Nasdaq lost 2.1% and the Dow added 296 points.

【中方強調捍衛《聯合國憲章》的重要性】中國常駐聯合國副代表孫磊23日在聯大第六委員會“《聯合國憲章》和加強聯合國作用特別委員會的報告”議題下發言，強調捍衛《聯合國憲章》的重要性。他說，《聯合國憲章》是當代國際法的堅實基礎，在國際法體系中具有最高效力，是處理國際關係的根本遵循。當前，國際形勢變亂交織。維護以聯合國為核心的國際體系、以國際法為基礎的國際秩序，確保憲章的宗旨和原則得到遵守和執行，比以往任何時候都更加迫切和必要。今年是東京審判開庭80週年，各方應以史為鑑，共同守護來之不易的和平成果和戰後秩序。孫磊表示，應踐行真正的多邊主義。無論大小、強弱、貧富，國家一律平等。國際上的事應由大家共同商量著辦，世界命運應由各國共同掌握。大國尤其要帶頭遵守憲章，旗幟鮮明反對單邊主義。中方反對未經安理會授權的單邊制裁，重申安理會制裁應以促進有關問題的政治解決為目標，以憲章宗旨和原則為遵循，以窮盡其他和平手段為前提，並最大程度降低對普通民眾和第三國的影響。（新華社）

【富國：中期選舉前宜押注美債上漲、做空美元】富國策略師建議，在11月3日中期選舉前押注美國久期資產上漲，並做空美元。“最可能出現的兩種結果，即民主黨橫掃兩院以及民主黨控制眾議院、共和黨控制參議院，都會帶來一定程度的（小幅）負面財政衝擊，”策略師Erik Nelson 、Angelo Manolatos和Francis Brown週五在報告中寫道，如果民主黨同時拿下國會兩院，可能利空AI主題交易，從而提振美國國債並打壓美元。策略師表示，特朗普威脅升級對伊朗的戰爭，對久期不利、對美元有利，因為這可能破壞該地區的能源基礎設施，並推高能源市場的風險溢價。報告稱，潛在的意外情景包括共和黨保住參議院，這將形成最強的政治掣肘，因而“最有可能帶來財政整固”，從而提振債券並拖累美元。策略師表示，如果共和黨表現強於預期緩解市場對AI遭遇抵制的擔憂，上述影響可能被抵消，最終影響可能為中性。

### 2026-10-24 01:10:27  #MKT News #Brent #Impact bullish #XAUUSD #DXY

【Report: Iran has detailed plan to strike Gulf undersea communications cables】Reuters: Two senior Iranian security officials said Iran has drawn up detailed plans to strike undersea communications cables in the Gulf if the US or Israel attacks, and could impede repairs to prolong outages. One official warned that if Iran is attacked "there will be no red lines", with submarine cables and military bases potentially at risk. The IRGC deputy for political affairs previously threatened strikes on seven main internet cables under the Strait of Hormuz, and a senior security official warned US-linked data centers and AI facilities in the Gulf could be targeted. Damage to cables could disrupt regional internet access, financial transactions, e‑commerce and cloud services. TeleGeography head of research Alan Mauldin said the Strait of Hormuz carries under 1% of global international bandwidth, so global impact is likely limited, but regional outages and wartime repair difficulties are material. The Red Sea is also a potential risk area, though Iran has not publicly said it would target cables there.

### 2026-10-23 23:56:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

NatGas Prices Fall to Almost 6-Month Low — US natural gas prices fell more than 5% on Friday to nearly $2.65 per MMBtu, approaching their lowest level since late April, as forecasts for milder weather weighed on expectations for heating demand. Above-average temperatures are expected across the Midwest and East through November 5, according to Commodity Weather Group, likely curbing gas consumption. The price decline came despite a smaller-than-usual storage build, strong LNG feedgas demand, and lower production. EIA data showed that US natural gas inventories rose by 77 Bcf in the week ended October 16, below both the 86 Bcf added during the same week last year and the five-year average increase of 87 Bcf. Meanwhile, average feedgas flows to the nine major US LNG export terminals climbed to 18.2 Bcf/d in October, up from 17.9 Bcf/d in September. On the supply side, production across the Lower 48 states averaged 111.4 Bcf/d this month, down from record highs of 113.3 Bcf/d in August and September.

Canadian Dollar Hits Over One-Month High — The Canadian dollar strengthened to around 1.40 per US dollar in October, reaching a more than one-month high as the outlook for BoC policy tilted toward tighter conditions. Expectations of a narrowing US-Canada interest rate differential have supported the loonie, amid a more hawkish outlook for the BoC’s next policy decision, scheduled for October 28th, relative to the US Fed. BoC Governor Tiff Macklem recently said the central bank expects to raise its quarterly inflation forecast significantly this month, following hawkish remarks last week about energy-driven price pressures. The oil rally has added to upward pressure on energy costs amid an escalation in the Middle East conflict. Meanwhile, Canadian retail sales are expected to have increased 0.7% month-over-month in September, extending a 1.2% rise in August, adding to the inflation outlook.

Brazilian Real Edges Lower Ahead of Presidential Runoff — The Brazilian real edged lower to around 5.045 per US dollar ahead of the presidential runoff, amid uncertainty surrounding the election. A Datafolha poll showed Senator Flávio Bolsonaro and President Lula tied at 50% each of valid votes. Earlier polls also indicated a statistical tie, with Bolsonaro’s lead narrowing compared with previous surveys. Some market participants believe a Flávio Bolsonaro administration would be more likely to rein in public spending, although neither the candidate nor his economic advisers have outlined concrete fiscal adjustments. Meanwhile, Brazil’s mid-month inflation rate reached 4.62% in October, below market expectations of 4.74%.

European Stocks End Week Lower — European stocks extended losses on Friday, with the Stoxx 50 falling around 0.5% to a more than four-month low and recording a weekly decline of over 2%. The broader Stoxx 600 also dropped 0.5% to a two-week low, ending the week down more than 1.5%. Germany, the UK and France led Friday’s declines, while Spain and Italy posted modest gains. Industrial goods and services, automobiles and parts, telecommunications, and consumer goods fell more than 1%, while banking, technology, travel and leisure, and media stocks also weakened. Energy stocks outperformed, gaining nearly 3% as Brent crude reached new highs amid escalating attacks on Saudi Arabia. Corporate earnings drove significant individual moves. Kering plunged more than 15% as Gucci sales declined, while Volvo Cars dropped nearly 25% following a sharp fall in Chinese sales. Safran lost 10% despite higher sales, whereas L’Oréal climbed over 5% on stronger-than-expected third-quarter results.

### 2026-10-23 22:49:00  #公司

【澳門特區政府舉行政府引導基金設立檔案簽署儀式】澳門特區政府23日舉行政府引導基金設立檔案簽署儀式，標誌基金正式運作。 儀式在特區政府總部舉行，由澳門特區行政長官岑浩輝、澳門中聯辦主任鄭新聰、特區政府經濟財政司司長吳惠嫻出席見證，澳門投資管理有限公司董事長謝永強與6家綜合旅遊休閒企業代表進行簽署。 據介紹，政府引導基金首期規模達200億澳門元，分別由特區政府以財政儲備歷年滾存收益出資110億澳門元，以及由6家綜合旅遊休閒企業作為首批社會資本合共出資90億澳門元組成。 基金按第11/2025號法律《投資基金法》及第26/2026號行政法規《政府引導基金管理制度》設立及運作，由澳門投資管理有限公司負責管理。基金貫徹長期主義及耐心資本的理念，採用“引導基金—母基金—子基金”三層架構，母基金及子基金由公開遴選的專業基金經理按市場化方式管理。 基金重點投向新興產業、產業轉型升級、科技成果轉化及初創科技企業等範疇，支援澳門與橫琴粵澳深度合作區、粵港澳大灣區其他城市聯動發展。(新華社)

### 2026-10-23 22:46:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Gold Slides Below $4,000 — Gold prices fell below $4,000 an ounce on Friday, hitting their lowest level since mid-July and putting the precious metal down almost 4% for the week, as rising oil prices strengthened expectations that US interest rates will remain higher for longer. Brent crude surged to a four-year high after Houthi attacks in Saudi Arabia heightened concerns over further energy supply disruptions. Meanwhile, recent US data showed consumer and producer inflation rising more than expected, largely driven by higher energy costs, reinforcing concerns over persistent inflationary pressures. Despite this, Federal Reserve officials are widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged at their October 27–28 meeting. Markets currently price in just a 10% chance of a rate hike next week, compared with odds of around 80% for December, according to CME’s FedWatch Tool. Investors now await US PCE inflation data due October 29 for further clues on progress towards the Fed’s 2% inflation target.

【國家郵政局召開郵政快遞業安全治理規範化座談會：要持續深入推進“反內卷”工作】10月22日至23日，國家郵政局在河南洛陽召開郵政快遞業安全治理規範化座談會。會議強調，要聚焦突出問題和關鍵環節，以更大力度更實舉措做好行業安全工作。一是大力整治生產安全突出隱患。要強化重大事故隱患排查整治，深入整治消防隱患，嚴格規範外包管理，持續完善企業安全管理體系。二是突出抓好寄遞渠道風險防範。要抓好寄遞安全“三項制度”落實，抓實抓細行業反詐工作，加強寄遞物品安全管理，加強資訊保安防範。三是強化事前事中事後全鏈條監管。要加強事前准入與事中事後管理銜接，抓好兩項強制性國家標準執行，加強協議使用者規範管理，提升廣大群眾用郵安全感。四是持續深入推進“反內卷”工作。要確保末端派費穩定，深化成本核算指導，嚴肅整治跨區攬件行為，企業總部要最佳化對所屬服務網路的考核管理規則，不得層層加碼、轉嫁經營成本。

### 2026-10-23 22:09:17  #資料 #市場 #國際

【美國企業活動保持擴張 價格創2022年以來最大漲幅】美國10月企業活動增速略有放緩，但仍接近多年高位，強勁需求推動新訂單、就業和價格上升。週五公佈的資料顯示，標普全球10月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)初值下降0.8點至57.6。這是2022年以來的第二高水平。指數高於50代表擴張。銷售價格指標以逾四年來最快速度上漲，反映能源、運輸和原材料成本大幅上升。標普全球市場財智的首席商業經濟學家Chris Williamson在宣告中表示：“成本壓力仍是主要擔憂，尤其是油價再度飆升的影響傳導至廣泛的商品和服務領域。”供應瓶頸也在惡化。供應商交貨時間出現2022年以來最大幅度的延長，標普全球指出，原因包括材料和勞動力短缺、供應商超負荷運轉以及運輸延誤。即便如此，Williamson表示，如果不考慮疫情後經濟重啟時期，美國企業正經歷2015年以來最強勁的一段增長期。強勁的消費者支出、AI推動的投資以及國防支出今年都為美國企業提供了支撐。衡量需求的新訂單指標10月創下四年半來最快增速。

### 2026-10-23 22:06:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

US Consumer Sentiment Revised Higher but Remains Weak — The University of Michigan’s consumer sentiment index was revised higher to 48.0 in late October 2026, up from a preliminary estimate of 46.3, but remained at its lowest level in five months. Gains in sentiment among Republicans and Democrats were offset by declines among independents. Year-ahead business conditions improved by around 14%, although the index remained 16% below its level in October 2025. Meanwhile, the decline in the Current Conditions subindex was driven entirely by buying conditions for durable goods, which plunged 18% amid higher interest rates and growing concerns about affordability. Purchasing power continues to dominate consumer views of the economy, with sentiment now 10% below its year-ago level and 15% below February, just before the Iran conflict. Year-ahead inflation expectations edged up to 4.7% from 4.6% in September, while long-run inflation expectations also increased, rising to 3.5% from 3.4%.

TSX Falls as Oil Rally Fuels BoC Rate Hike Bets — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell more than 0.5% to hover near 35,500 on Friday as the oil rally continued to fuel inflation concerns, while technology stocks remained under pressure. Crude oil prices climbed amid escalating Houthi attacks on Saudi targets and reports of possible renewed US strikes against Iran. Canadian bond yields moved higher, with markets pricing in a Bank of Canada rate hike at its next meeting amid energy-driven inflationary pressures. Major Canadian banks posted losses, with RBC, TD Bank, BMO, Scotiabank and CIBC each down about 1%. Meanwhile, technology stocks remained under pressure as the rotation away from AI-related shares continued. Celestica extended its losses, falling more than 3.5% after plunging 12% in the previous session, while Shopify shed nearly 1%. Celestica is scheduled to report its third-quarter 2026 results on Monday. Elsewhere, gold prices fluctuated and silver fell, weighing on mining stocks. On the week, the TSX shed near 2%.

### 2026-10-23 21:59:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Services PMI #Importance 1

US Services Activity Growth Remains Robust — The S&P Global US Services PMI eased to 57.9 in October 2026 from 58.8 in September and marginally below market forecasts of 58, according to a flash estimate. The latest reading marked the seventh straight month of growth in business activity, with the pace of expansion remaining among the strongest since 2011, despite a slight moderation from September. New business growth accelerated to its fastest pace since March 2022, supported by stronger domestic demand, while export orders recorded their largest increase since July 2023. Employment growth moderated compared with September but was still the third largest for over four years. However, price pressures intensified, with services inflation reaching its highest level since August 2022. This reflected a further sharp increase in business costs, driven by rising fuels, alongside higher wages and rising utility and insurance expenses. Business output expectations for the 12 months ahead cooled down from September's one-year high.

Ibovespa Fluctuates Ahead of Presidential Runoff — The Ibovespa fluctuated around the 204,000 mark on Friday in the final session before Brazil’s presidential runoff. A Datafolha poll showed Senator Flávio Bolsonaro and President Lula tied at 50% each of valid votes. Earlier polls also indicated a statistical tie, with Bolsonaro’s lead narrowing compared with previous surveys. Some market participants believe a Flávio Bolsonaro administration would be more likely to rein in public spending, although neither the candidate nor his economic advisers have outlined concrete fiscal adjustments. Financial stocks mostly traded lower, with Itaú edging down and Banco do Brasil and Itaúsa losing more than 0.5%. Bradesco fell nearly 2.5% despite reporting third-quarter earnings in line with market expectations and announcing a share buyback program. In contrast, Petrobras rose nearly 2% as oil prices climbed following fresh attacks in the Middle East. Vale gained nearly 1% as iron ore prices rebounded. On the week, the Ibovespa shed over 0.5%.

US Business Activity Growth Holds Close to 5-Year High — The US Composite PMI eased to 57.6 in October 2026 from 58.4 in September, a two-month low, but remained firmly in expansion territory and signalled the second-strongest increase in business activity since March 2022. Growth in new orders strengthened to its highest level in more than four-and-a-half years, while export sales recorded their largest gain since May 2022. Employment also remained strong, rising at its fastest pace since June 2022 as companies increased staffing to meet demand and address growing backlogs. However, mounting cost pressures drove selling-price inflation to its highest level since August 2022. Input costs rose at their fastest pace since November 2022, excluding September’s increase. Meanwhile, business confidence for the year ahead weakened slightly but remained the second-highest reading recorded this year.

Baltic Dry Index Snaps 4-Day Winning Run — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, snapped its four-day winning streak on Friday, falling by 1.2% to 2,660 points. The panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, dropped roughly 2% to a fresh low since August 24 at 2,109 points. Meanwhile, the capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, also halted its four-day winning run, decreasing by 1.5% to 3,791 points. Among smaller vessels, the supramax index went down by 0.2% to 1,887 points. For the week, the benchmark index advanced about 5.9%, notching its second consecutive weekly gain, mainly supported by the bigger-size segment.

### 2026-10-23 21:15:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Belgium #Business Confidence #Importance 0

Belgium Business Pessimism Eases to Lowest Since January — Belgium’s business confidence indicator rose by 2.3 points to -9.8 in October 2026, the highest reading since January, compared to -12.1 in September and above market expectations of -11.8. Manufacturing sentiment became less pessimistic (-9.2 vs. -12.2), reflecting less negative assessments of total order books (-18.6 vs. -20.2) and employment expectations (-6.8 vs. -7.4), alongside a decline in stock levels (0.5 vs. 10.4). The confidence index also strengthened in the building industry (-8.3 vs -11.8), led by the trend in orders (0.5 vs -6.9) and the assessment of order books (-20.4 vs -24.5), as well as in business-related services (-12.6 vs -13.4), driven by activity expectations (-4.1 vs -7.4). In contrast, pessimism deepened in the trade sector (-13.2 vs -8.6), with employment expectations falling sharply (-13.0 vs -1.4).

Silver Falls to 2025 Lows — Silver prices tumbled below $54 an ounce on Friday, reaching their lowest level since November 2025 as rising oil prices strengthened expectations that US interest rates will remain higher for longer. Brent crude climbed to a four-year high after Houthi attacks in Saudi Arabia heightened concerns over further energy supply disruptions. Meanwhile, hotter-than-expected US consumer and producer inflation data last week, largely driven by rising energy costs, reinforced the case for tighter monetary policy. However, the Fed is widely expected to leave rates unchanged at its October 27–28 meeting. Markets currently price in just a 10% chance of a rate hike next week, compared with 80% for December, according to CME’s FedWatch Tool. Investors are also awaiting US PCE inflation data on October 29 for further clues on progress towards the Fed’s 2% target.

Canada Retail Sales Rise for 2nd Month — Retail sales in Canada likely increased 0.7% month-over-month in September 2026, extending a 1.2% rise in August, according to preliminary estimates. Meanwhile, data for August was revised down from a preliminary estimate of a 1.3% increase. In August, the largest gain in retail sales was observed at motor vehicle and parts dealers (+1.2%), led by new car dealers (1.5%). In August, gains at automotive parts, accessories and tire retailers (+1.4%) were offset by lower sales at other motor vehicle dealers (-1.4%) and used car dealers (-0.5%). ### 2026-10-23 20:07:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Monthly GDP YoY #Importance 1

Mexican Economic Growth Beats Forecasts in August — Mexico’s economic activity rose 3.3% year over year in August 2026, easing slightly from 3.4% growth in July but exceeding forecasts of 3.1%. Tertiary activities led the expansion, growing 3.6%, supported by wholesale trade (12.1%), other services excluding government activities (7.3%) and information services in mass media (5.0%). Secondary activities increased 3.5%, driven by construction (7.5%) and manufacturing (3.2%). In contrast, primary activities contracted 2.6%, as agricultural activity fell 6.1%. Year to date, economic activity grew 1.7%.

【SAFE seeks comment on lowering category risk conversion factor to 0.5 for affiliate foreign borrowing】The State Administration of Foreign Exchange (SAFE) published a draft notice seeking comments on measures to further ease cross-border investment and financing facilitation. Under the draft’s macroprudential management framework, qualified foreign‑invested enterprises that borrow foreign debt from overseas affiliates would see their category risk conversion factor reduced from 1 to 0.5. SAFE also proposes a nationwide rollout of a green foreign‑debt policy: under macroprudential management, eligible borrowers using foreign debt for green development or low‑carbon transition projects would likewise have the category risk conversion factor cut from 1 to 0.5.

【SAFE consults on draft: overseas-investment standby funds remittable up to $200,000/month, capped at 10% of registered ODI remittance quota】The State Administration of Foreign Exchange (SAFE) published a draft notice seeking comments on measures to further ease cross-border investment and financing. The draft permits domestic institutions to remit funds abroad as overseas-investment standby funds for small, routine operating expenses of overseas investees (rent, utilities, staff costs, office, transport, taxes, petty procurement). Banks may process remittances on presentation of a commitment letter specifying offshore use. Remittances are limited to the equivalent of $200,000 per month and cumulatively may not exceed 10% of the domestic institution’s registered outbound direct investment (ODI) remittance quota. Misuse of standby funds will be investigated and dealt with according to law.

US 10-Year Treasury Yield at 5% — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note rose to 5% on Friday, following a 17bps drop in the previous session, which was the largest single-day decline in more than a year. Despite the modest rebound, cautious sentiment persisted as oil prices climbed further amid concerns over a potential escalation of hostilities in the Middle East, with the US midterm elections also less than two weeks away. Meanwhile, concerns over heavy AI-related spending continued to weigh on sentiment. The Federal Reserve is set to announce its monetary policy decision next week, with markets pricing in a roughly 91% probability of rates remaining unchanged. Meanwhile, the odds of a 25bps rate hike in December stood at around 76%.

### 2026-10-23 19:41:05  #宏觀

【李強主持召開國務院常務會議 研究統籌人口與經濟社會發展有關工作 聽取推進全國服務業大會部署落實情況彙報】國務院總理李強10月23日主持召開國務院常務會議，研究統籌人口與經濟社會發展有關工作，聽取推進全國服務業大會部署落實情況彙報。

會議指出，人口是經濟社會發展的基礎條件，要更好統籌人口與經濟社會、資源環境的關係，進一步完善相關政策，持續積累和釋放人口綜合紅利，以人口高質量發展更好支撐經濟社會高質量發展。要更加突出人的全面發展，提高人口科學文化、職業技能、身心健康等水平，加快完善全民終身學習機制，廣泛開展技能培訓，強化教育、培訓與產業發展協同聯動，促進高質量充分就業。要加強區域人口發展分類指導，引導城鄉人口有序流動，穩步推進基本公共服務均等化，根據人口分佈情況最佳化基礎設施佈局，推動公共服務資源與常住人口變化動態適配。要加快建設生育友好型社會，持續完善婚嫁、生育、養育、教育等政策，最佳化面向家庭的財稅、社保、住房等支援舉措，努力穩定新出生人口規模。

會議指出，要進一步發揮服務業對經濟發展的支撐作用，加力推進服務業擴能提質，把服務業潛力空間轉化為發展動能。要加快破解服務業發展的難點堵點，聚焦生產生活重點領域因業施策，加強融資、用地、人才、資料等支援，穩步推進公共事業領域價格改革，加大力度盤活用好閒置資源。要按照建設全國統一大市場的要求，持續破除市場準入障礙，清理民間投資隱性壁壘，加快構建與新業態發展相適應的監管方式。要充分調動各方面發展服務業的積極性，加強分類指導和督促協調，增強政策針對性有效性，支援有條件的地方先行先試，因地制宜探索服務業創新發展路徑。要鼓勵擴大服務業投資，釋放服務消費潛力，培育龍頭企業和特色品牌。要進一步健全適應服務業發展的統計監測體系，強化上下聯動、齊抓共管的工作格局。

會議還研究了其他事項。（新華社）

【法國國債本週經歷歷史性拋售 市場關注穆迪是否會下調該國評級】法國債券市場本週經歷了歷史性動盪。隨著政治局勢不安可能進一步加劇市場對該國公共財政的擔憂，一項備受關注的風險指標正接近有記錄以來最大升幅。資料顯示，本週迄今，投資者持有法國10年期國債而非德國同期限國債所要求的風險溢價已上升33個基點，為1990年有記錄以來最大升幅。法國10年期國債收益率週五企穩在5%左右，CAC 40指數則下跌。投資者擔心，在債務負擔沉重、經濟增長乏力，以及公眾不滿演變成混亂街頭示威的背景下，法國正面臨危險的前景。市場對本週末再度爆發抗議活動的擔憂，也令企業高管在週末前保持警惕。更廣泛而言，隨著油價升至每桶100美元上方，加之市場對AI盈利能力感到不安，全球風險資產均遭到拋售。

### 2026-10-23 19:25:54  #其他

【華福證券陳興：建議投資者規避短期情緒干擾，避免恐慌式離場】華福證券宏觀首席分析師陳興接受證券時報記者採訪時表示，此前市場調整，主要受美債收益率高位執行、海外AI融資預期走弱帶來的外部估值壓制影響，國內基本面並未出現惡化。9月下旬指數加速回撥後，市場情緒與投資者倉位已回落至低位。伴隨三季報披露進入尾聲，盈利修復拐點有望進一步確認，疊加增量資金持續進場，市場或將逐步消化外部擾動，迴歸基本面定價主線。陳興建議投資者規避短期情緒干擾，避免恐慌式離場，聚焦業績與估值匹配度較好的優質標的，保持理性、耐心佈局。陳興表示，從宏觀基本面看，我國經濟呈現“總量穩、動能新、邊際改善”的積極特徵。結構上，第三產業增加值同比增長5.2%，成為穩增長的核心支撐，“新興領域良性迴圈加快形成”，高技術產業與新質生產力對增長的貢獻持續提升。整體來看，三季度經濟環比提速、景氣邊際回升，一定程度上打破了前期市場對於基本面偏弱的悲觀預期，也為後續企業盈利兌現、市場估值修復奠定了基本面基礎。

【DeepSeek完成約1000億元新融資 產業資本攜資源密集入局】記者從多個一級投資人處確認，DeepSeek近日已完成約1000億元人民幣（約150億美元）融資，重新整理中國AI行業單輪融資紀錄。據悉，本輪融資目標由前期的800億元上調至1000億元。老股東寧德時代、騰訊控股作為主要投資方參與本輪融資，IDG資本、礪思資本及拾象資本繼續跟投。新晉投資方多為產業資本，包括聯想系君聯資本、兆易創新系石溪資本、吉利汽車產業基金、中芯聚源，以及後期加入的橫店資本等。針對本輪融資的產業邏輯，一名投資人士向證券時報記者表示，當前大模型的發展本質上是工程問題，底層架構已無明顯秘密，核心壁壘在於訓練資料集與算力資源。因此，DeepSeek在此次融資中對投資方提出了明確的資源協同要求，要求所有意向投資方必須說明能夠為DeepSeek提供何種資料支援與業務協同。在此導向下，具備深厚產業資源與協同能力的機構獲得了更多份額，例如橫店資本等獲得了較大額度的投資份額。（證券時報）

### 2026-10-23 19:00:51  #其他

【重慶市璧山區人大常委會原黨組副書記朱朝陽被“雙開”】中央紀委國家監委網站10月23日通報，據重慶市紀委監委訊息：經重慶市委批准，重慶市紀委監委對璧山區人大常委會原黨組副書記、副主任朱朝陽嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，朱朝陽喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，官迷心竅、利令智昏，結交政治騙子，對抗組織審查，搞迷信活動；無視中央八項規定精神，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請和旅遊安排；違反組織原則，不按規定報告個人有關事項，在職工錄用、工作調動中為他人謀利並收受財物；廉潔底線失守，違規收受禮品禮金，違規從事營利活動，違規借用管理和服務物件錢款車輛；違規干預和插手司法活動；私慾膨脹，利用職務便利為他人在土地出讓、工程承攬、企業經營等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。朱朝陽嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經重慶市紀委常委會會議研究並報重慶市委批准，決定給予朱朝陽開除黨籍處分；由重慶市監委給予其開除公職處分；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

【專家：伊朗為與美談判留有空間】伊朗極少公佈最高領袖穆傑塔巴·哈梅內伊線下會面的訊息，他上一次被報道的面對面會見，還是今年8月與伊朗總統佩澤希齊揚會晤，隨後伊朗國內啟動一輪人事調整。如今，在美以醞釀新一輪打擊的關鍵節點，穆傑塔巴10月21日與伊朗議長、首席談判代表卡利巴夫的會見對外公開，傳遞出哪些關鍵訊號？中國現代國際關係研究院中東研究所副所長秦天分析指出，穆傑塔巴相關活動的公佈往往是在一些重要時機，此次會見卡利巴夫是要處理三方面重要問題：其一，是備戰打仗的問題，以應對美國中期選舉後，美以醞釀的新軍事進攻；其二，此次會面也是伊朗最高領袖鞏固個人權威的一個步驟，顯示其仍在掌控局面，在關鍵重大問題上決定國家方向；其三，也是為與美國談判留有靈活空間——美以伊衝突爆發以來，卡利巴夫在伊朗政壇上代表了與美國談判的立場。穆傑塔巴選擇與其會見，而非伊朗總統或革命衛隊領導人，可能是有意在緊張局勢下，探索談判的可能性。 （CCTV國際時訊）

### 2026-10-23 18:47:45  #央行 #公司 #市場

【大同證券宏觀策略分析師景劍文：受情緒影響產生的非理性下跌無需過於恐慌，科技板塊中長期發展趨勢依然向好】10月23日，大同證券宏觀策略分析師景劍文表示，當前市場短期波動主要受恐慌情緒主導，投資者對利空訊息的敏感度上升，放大了部分利空因素的影響，導致出現一定程度的非理性下跌。然而，理性審視下，這些利空因素進一步惡化的可能性相對偏低，情緒面衝擊更多是階段性的。與此同時，諸多利好因素正在疊加積聚，金融街論壇召開，央行、金融監管總局、證監會密集發聲，多項穩定市場舉措有望落地；市場層面亦有增量資金進場，多隻ETF現大額資金流入；三季報也有科技板塊公司展現亮眼業績。多重利好有助於緩解近期市場持續下挫的恐慌情緒。景劍文認為，中長期看，市場仍有回暖基礎。宏觀層面，全球環境未顯著惡化，國內經濟資料表現穩定，經濟韌性為市場中長期穩定發展提供支撐。科技板塊雖經歷較大回撥，但估值顯著回落、風險出清程度較高，全球科技競賽持續、大廠資本開支未見縮減，AI應用推動經營閉環逐步穩定，中長期發展趨勢依然向好。他表示，短期受情緒影響產生的非理性下跌無需過於恐慌，反而科技板塊在持續回落、出清風險後，有望重回主線帶動市場上行，放眼長期，或可逢低佈局科技相關板塊。

### 2026-10-23 18:46:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Russia #Currency #Importance 0

Russian Ruble Hovers at 3-Month High — The Russian ruble hovered around 81 per US dollar on Friday, reaching its strongest level in nearly three months after the Bank of Russia kept its key interest rate unchanged at 14%, in line with market expectations. The central bank maintained a cautious stance as persistent inflationary pressures and elevated expectations continue to complicate its efforts to bring inflation back to the 4% target. Annual inflation stood at an estimated 6.8% as of October 19, while the bank expects it to reach 7.1–7.6% by year-end. Ukrainian attacks on Russian energy infrastructure have disrupted refinery operations, contributed to fuel shortages and increased transportation costs, adding to price pressures. The central bank also warned that capacity constraints and wage growth exceeding productivity gains pose further inflation risks. Policymakers forecast an average key rate of 12.5–13.5% in 2027 and expect inflation to return to target that year.

### 2026-10-23 18:40:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Sensex Finishes Week Lower — India’s BSE Sensex closed about 0.2% down at an over one-week low of 73,124 on Friday, marking the fourth straight session of losses and posting a 2.1% weekly drop. Market sentiment continued to be dominated by concerns over rising oil prices, geopolitical tensions and uncertainty regarding future developments. High oil prices pose risks to India’s import bill, inflation outlook, and corporate margins. Adding to the pressure, foreign institutional investors sold Indian equities worth 3.46 billion rupees on October 22. Meanwhile, flash PMI data showed Indian private sector growth accelerated to a four-month high in October. Selling remained widespread across sectors, with metal and consumer durables stocks among the biggest laggards. Tata Steel led the losses, falling 3.2%, followed by Adani Ports, which declined 3.03%. Meanwhile, Eternal recorded the strongest performance, gaining roughly 4%. Larsen & Toubro advanced 1.3%, Reliance Industries rose 1.2%, and TCS increased by 1%.

### 2026-10-23 18:34:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Russia #Interest Rate #Importance 2

Russia Holds Interest Rate as Expected — The Bank of Russia kept its key interest rate unchanged at 14% in October, in line with market expectations, as inflationary pressures and elevated expectations continued to hinder disinflation. Ukrainian strikes on Russian energy infrastructure disrupted refinery operations, pushed crude-processing rates to multi-year lows and contributed to fuel shortages, raising gasoline prices and transportation costs across the economy. Inflation accelerated to an annualized 8% in Q3, from 4.8% in Q2, while core inflation rose to 6.2% from 4.5%. Annual inflation stood at an estimated 6.8% as of October 19 and is expected to reach 7.1–7.6% by year-end, above the central bank’s earlier forecast. The central bank said capacity constraints, strong wage growth and persistent inflation expectations pose significant risks, with proinflationary factors outweighing disinflationary ones. It expects inflation to return to its 4% target in 2027, projecting an average key rate of 12.5–13.5% that year.

【Research on Governance Framework for Intelligent Connected New Energy Vehicle Industry formally released】Oct 23 — At the closing of the 2026 World Intelligent Connected Vehicles Conference in Beijing, jointly hosted by the Ministry of Industry and Information Technology, the Ministry of Transport and the Beijing municipal government, the report Research on the Governance Framework for the Intelligent Connected New Energy Vehicle Industry under New Conditions was formally released. Led by the Equipment Industry Development Center and produced by a 13‑member team of OEMs, tech firms and academic institutions over one year, the report defines a seven‑element governance framework — objectives, principles, governed entities, stakeholders, tools, mechanisms and outcomes — and broadens governance from whole vehicles to algorithmic models, charging and battery‑swap facilities and software upgrade systems, while extending the governance chain across the full product lifecycle to better accommodate rapid technology iteration.

### 2026-10-23 18:28:27  #其他

【《新形勢下智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理體系框架研究》正式釋出】10月23日，由工業和資訊化部、交通運輸部、北京市人民政府聯合主辦的2026世界智慧網聯汽車大會閉幕式在北京召開。會上，《新形勢下智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理體系框架研究》（以下簡稱《報告》）正式釋出。《報告》由裝備工業發展中心牽頭，聯合整車企業、科技企業、高校機構等13家單位組成課題組，歷時一年完成。《報告》構建了涵蓋治理目標、原則、客體、主體、工具、機制和效果七個要素的智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理體系框架，將治理範圍從整車產品延伸至演算法模型、充換電設施、軟體升級系統等領域，將治理鏈條拓展到產品全生命週期，以更好適應技術快速迭代的特點。同時，《報告》從戰略引導與制度供給、跨部門跨央地協同、數字監管與風險防控、市場規範與社會共治、國際規則制定與協調五個方面提出能力提升路徑，推動制度在實踐中迭代完善，逐步形成創新協同、安全高效、持續演進、動態最佳化的行業現代化治理能力。下一步，裝備工業發展中心將持續深化智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理研究，與各方共同推動智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理體系和治理能力現代化，在全球規則制定與協調中積極貢獻中國方案。

Bund Yields Near Two-Month Lows — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield eased to around 3.34%, touching its lowest level since August 31, as investors sought safe-haven assets amid mounting fiscal concerns and political uncertainty in France and other highly indebted eurozone economies. Meanwhile, fresh PMI data showed eurozone private-sector growth slowed to a four-month low in October, pressured by rising energy costs, higher interest rates and renewed contraction in France. Consumer confidence also deteriorated more sharply than expected. French bonds remained under particular pressure, with the spread between French OATs and German Bunds widening to around 170 basis points, its highest level since the eurozone sovereign debt crisis. Looking ahead, further demonstrations are expected across the region this weekend, while investors await next week’s inflation data and the ECB’s policy meeting, where officials are widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged.

UK Gilt Yields Hold Steady as Investors Await Budget — UK 10-year gilt yields held steady around 5.33% as investors assessed mixed economic data ahead of next week’s budget. UK assets, including gilts and sterling, have attracted stronger demand as concerns over social unrest and mounting debt across the eurozone prompted investors to shift away from other regional markets. Flash PMI data showed business activity accelerated to an eight-month high, beating forecasts, while retail sales fell more sharply than expected in September and consumer confidence suffered a steep decline in October. Stronger-than-expected inflation data earlier this week fueled expectations of further monetary tightening, with markets pricing in at least three 25bp rate hikes by April 2027 and the first potentially coming in November. Ahead of next week’s budget, investors appear to be giving Chancellor John Healey the benefit of the doubt, with gilt markets remaining unusually calm despite concerns over ambitious spending plans that have yet to be fully costed.

Slovenia Tourist Arrivals Hit Record for a September — Tourist arrivals in Slovenia rose by 11.4% year-on-year to 821.8 thousand in September 2026, the highest September level on record, following a 6.0% rise in the previous month. Foreign tourist arrivals increased by 12.8% to 682.8 thousand, with visitors mainly coming from the UK (39.5%), the US (27.5%), the Czech Republic (26.5%), Germany (12.8%), and Austria (9.5%). Meanwhile, domestic tourism grew by 4.7% to 139.0 thousand. Overnight stays went up by 12.4% to 1,994.2 thousand, with most tourists staying in mountain municipalities (22.6%), other municipalities (10.1%), urban municipalities (8.5%), and the capital (7.7%). For the January–September period, tourist arrivals increased by 7.1% year-on-year to 6,212.3 thousand, while overnight stays also rose by 7.1% to 16,072.6 thousand.

From January to September, total visitor arrivals rose 8.5% year-on-year to 32.181 million.

### 2026-10-23 16:02:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Nickel Retreats on Persistent Oversupply — Nickel traded around $15,900 per tonne, reversing recent gains as persistent global oversupply and subdued demand continued to constrain prices. Indonesia’s rapid expansion of nickel production has kept supply ample, while weaker stainless steel demand and the growing use of lithium iron phosphate batteries have limited consumption growth. The International Nickel Study Group expects the global surplus to widen in 2027, reinforcing the longer-term supply overhang. Meanwhile, reports of coordinated production cuts at Chinese-operated Indonesian smelters have raised hopes of tighter supply, with producers reportedly considering halving mixed hydroxide precipitate (MHP) output and cutting nickel pig iron production by 20%. Although the scale and timing of the measures remain uncertain, losses among Indonesian MHP producers strengthen the incentive to curb output, potentially tightening supply if the cuts are implemented.

### 2026-10-23 15:51:41  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

UK Gas Prices Hit Fresh Multi-Year High Amid Tight Supply — UK natural gas prices climbed toward 230 pence per therm, reaching a fresh 2022 high amid tightening global supply. Ongoing disruptions to LNG supplies from the Middle East have driven British gas prices to three times their levels at the start of the year. Adding to concerns, gas output in the North Sea, which accounts for about a third of the nation’s winter demand, is projected to fall by about 5%, according to the UK’s gas-grid operator. To compensate for this, LNG imports are forecast to increase by 5%, while Norwegian pipeline deliveries are expected to rise by 1%. While the gas network operator indicated that the country would have adequate supply capacity even under worse-case scenarios, robust LNG demand could intensify competition for cargoes and push prices even higher. Meanwhile, additional production from the newly approved Jackdaw field may offer some relief, as it is expected to start supplying gas to the UK by the end of this month.

Poland Jobless Rate Unchanged for 3rd Month — Poland’s registered unemployment rate stood at 5.8% in September 2026, in line with market expectations and unchanged for a third consecutive month. The latest result still marked the lowest level since December 2025, as the number of unemployed persons declined by 2.5 thousand from a month earlier to 907 thousand, with women representing 49.5% of the total. The majority of unemployed individuals were not entitled to unemployment benefits, accounting for 87.8% of the total. Meanwhile, 4.5% were out of work due to company-related terminations, while fresh graduates made up 3.4% of all unemployed persons. Additionally, 382.1 thousand had been out of work for more than a year. Compared with September 2025, the jobless rate was 0.2 percentage points higher, having stood at 5.6% a year earlier. Separately, reported job vacancies at the end of the month increased by 2.8 thousand to 47.2 thousand.

### 2026-10-23 15:28:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Stock Market #Importance 2

European Stocks Lower to End the Week — European stocks edged lower on Friday, with the STOXX 50 falling 0.3% and the STOXX 600 declining nearly 0.1%, as investors weighed rising oil prices amid escalating Middle East tensions against fresh corporate earnings updates. Kering tumbled almost 11% as Gucci sales dropped. Volvo Cars plunged nearly 25% following a sharp decline in sales in China. Safran lost 5.3% despite reporting a rise in sales. SAP was also down (-2.1%). In contrast, L'Oreal was up more than 3% after the company reported upbeat sales for Q3. SAAB shares rose 1.5% after the defence company reported better-than-expected earnings and revenue, supported by a deal to supply Gripen fighter jets to Ukraine. Meanwhile, flash PMI data showed a slowdown in private sector growth in both the Eurozone and Germany while French private-sector activity contracted. For the week, the EURO STOXX 50 lost around 1.9%, while the STOXX Europe 600 fell approximately 1%.

### 2026-10-23 15:25:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Composite PMI #Importance 1

France Private Sector Activity Returns to Contraction — France’s S&P Global Composite PMI fell to 48.4 in October 2026 from 51.1 in September, largely missing market forecasts of 50.5, flash estimates showed. This marked a return to contraction after September’s rebound, with activity at a four-month low. New orders fell the most since May as domestic and global uncertainty prompted customers to delay projects and curb spending. Export orders also continued to shrink for an eleventh consecutive month. Moreover, employment declined for a sixth straight month as companies reduced headcounts through non-replacement of departing workers and contract non-renewals. On the price front, overall inflationary pressures eased as slower services inflation offset rising manufacturing costs, with supply-chain disruptions driving the sharpest increase in factory selling prices since May. Looking ahead, business confidence deteriorated to its lowest level since April 2020, with firms citing concerns over next year’s elections and public finances.

---

### 2026-10-23 15:24:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

France Manufacturing Returns to Contraction in October — The S&P Global France Manufacturing PMI fell to 49.3 in October 2026 from 50.6 in September, slipping back into contraction after two months of expansion and reaching an 11-month low, preliminary estimates showed. The reading also came below market expectations of 50.5, as the manufacturing output index declined to 48.5 from 50.3, signaling a renewed contraction in production. Weaker demand and heightened economic uncertainty weighed on activity, while worsening supply-chain conditions drove manufacturing price pressures higher.

### 2026-10-23 15:17:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kazakhstan #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Kazakhstan Unexpectedly Cuts Rates Again by 50 Bps — The National Bank of Kazakhstan lowered its base rate to 15.75% on October 23, 2026, surprising markets that had widely expected a 25 bps cut. The decision followed September’s unexpected quarter-point reduction, as annual inflation eased for a twelfth consecutive month to 9.3% amid a tight monetary policy and favorable dynamics of the tenge exchange rate. However, services inflation remained elevated at 8.9%, highlighting persistent price pressures. The NBK warned that risks remained tilted toward higher inflation, citing elevated global energy and food prices, tighter global monetary conditions and strong domestic demand. The economy grew 4.2% year over year in January-September, supported by construction, manufacturing and transport, while fiscal stimulus and robust investment continued to underpin activity. The central bank said the cut preserves moderately tight monetary conditions, with further easing unlikely before year-end. Its next rate decision is scheduled for December 4.

### 2026-10-23 15:16:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

India 10Y Yield Extends Gains as Oil Prices Surge — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec edged higher to around 7.46%, extending gains for another session as surging oil prices weighed on investor sentiment, while falling U.S. Treasury yields limited the increase. Brent crude climbed above USD 114 a barrel to a five-month high following Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia, raising concerns over supply disruptions. As India imports nearly 90% of its oil requirements, sustained price increases threaten to widen the import bill, fuel inflation, pressure the rupee, and weaken public finances. Meanwhile, investors remained cautious ahead of a INR 330 billion government debt sale, keeping demand subdued. The benchmark 6.94% 2036 bond yield rose, nearing the 7.50% level. However, U.S. Treasury yields fell below 5% amid weaker economic data, an equity selloff, and concerns over the returns on AI spending and its growth implications.

【中國恢復聯合國合法席位55週年，外交部：將在新歷史起點上繼續發揮負責任大國作用】10月23日，外交部發言人毛寧主持例行記者會。有記者提問，10月25日是新中國恢復聯合國合法席位55週年紀念日，請問中方如何評價55年來的歷程？未來將如何推進同聯合國合作？毛寧表示，1971年10月25日，第二十六屆聯大以壓倒性多數透過第2758號決議，決定恢復中華人民共和國在聯合國的一切權力，這標誌著佔世界人口1/4的中國人民重返聯合國舞臺，對中國、對世界、對聯合國都具有重要而深遠的意義。中國人民銘記為此向我們提供寶貴支援和幫助的友好國家和人士。55年來，中國堅持做世界和平的建設者、全球發展的貢獻者、國際秩序的維護者，我們堅定支援聯合國在國際事務中發揮核心作用，積極參與聯合國各領域工作，忠實履行安理會常任理事國職責。我們立足聯合國平臺，同各國加強團結合作，倡導共同安全，推動共同發展，促進文明對話，改革完善全球治理。站在新的歷史起點上，中國將繼續發揮負責任大國作用，持續深化同聯合國的全方位合作，共同應對全球性挑戰，攜手構建人類命運共同體。（澎湃新聞）

【美國汽油價格居高不下 中期選舉前將維持高位】高油價持續加重美國民眾經濟負擔，在11月初中期選舉來臨前，選民將生活負擔能力列為首要關切問題。 美國汽車協會（AAA）的資料顯示，全美汽油均價為每加侖4.27美元；自2月伊朗戰爭爆發以來，油價漲幅43%。 部分分析師表示，11月3日選舉日之前燃油價格很難大幅回落，幾乎肯定會維持在每加侖4美元以上。 分析師認為，油價居高不下源於中東持續戰事：戰爭損毀石油基礎設施，迫使企業採用成本高昂的原油運輸方式。此外，俄烏衝突同樣限制了俄羅斯的燃油出口，進一步加劇供應短缺。 分析師稱，目前沒有跡象表明上述兩場衝突會很快結束。他們補充說，就算中東達成和平協議、原油成本回落，汽油價格也會滯後下跌——零售商要先賣完高價時期採購的成品油庫存。

South Korean Shares Close Higher — The benchmark KOSPI edged up 0.30% to close at 5,550 on Friday, recovering slightly from the previous session's plunge, as investors bought beaten-down semiconductor stocks. SK hynix advanced 4.42%, while Samsung Electronics rose 0.47%, alongside gains in SK Square (3.20%) and Samsung Biologics (7.71%). Retail investors also turned net buyers during the session, signaling a shift in sentiment after a sharp selloff. However, lingering concerns over returns on massive AI-related investments, compounded by overnight losses in major US technology stocks, limited the rebound. Losses in LG Energy Solution (-1.79%), Hyundai Motor (-1.99%), and KB Financial Group (-3.29%) also weighed on the index, highlighting uneven performance across large-cap stocks. At the same time, elevated oil prices amid continued Middle East tensions remained a risk, as higher energy costs could stoke inflation and increase operating expenses.

【SK海力士：會長給出“持有”建議三個月後，散戶虧損比例達74%】大約三個月前，SK集團會長崔泰源對SK海力士股票給出了“持有”的建議。但該股當前價格依舊較高點回落近60%。不少在高位附近入場的散戶深度套牢，陷入既不敢割肉、也無力加倉的境地。高位集中買入的散戶成為虧損主力。4–6月是該股行情高點區間，個人投資者淨買入規模合計31.5萬億韓元；其中6月股價創出歷史新高，單月散戶淨買入15.63萬億韓元。即便7月股價從250萬韓元區間暴跌至130萬韓元，散戶依舊淨買入9.001萬億韓元，不斷攤薄持倉成本。但後續股價進一步下探至120萬韓元附近，攤薄操作也沒能讓他們避開虧損。NH投資證券資料顯示，截至10月19日，持有SK海力士的投資者當中，74.18%處於虧損狀態。美林證券稱，按週三收盤價測算，該股明年預期市淨率僅1.10倍，處於歷史低位；如果出臺力度更強的股東回報措施，有望凸顯估值窪地。

Japanese Stocks Pare Losses as Tech Selloff Eases — Japan’s Nikkei 225 Index fell as much as 2.3% before recovering to close 0.34% lower at 62,693 on Friday, as selling pressure in technology and artificial intelligence stocks began to ease. Despite the late-session recovery, the benchmark index declined 4% for the week, marking its second consecutive weekly loss as investors shifted away from high-performing tech and AI shares toward safer assets, including government bonds. Market sentiment was also weighed down by escalating tensions in the Middle East, following reports that the US and Israel had prepared plans for possible renewed strikes against Iran after next month’s midterm elections. Several major index constituents remained under pressure, including Taiyo Yuden (-3.9%), Disco Corp (-3.8%), Mitsubishi UFJ (-2.3%), Hitachi (-4.4%) and Nintendo (-1.8%). Meanwhile, Japan’s inflation accelerated in September, rising above the Bank of Japan’s 2% target and reinforcing expectations of another interest rate hike this year.

### 2026-10-23 14:52:51  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Currency #Importance 1

Rupiah Extends Weekly Losses Despite Softer Dollar — The Indonesian rupiah held near IDR 18,080 per U.S. dollar on Friday, remaining above the 18,000 mark for a third session despite broad weakness in the greenback. The dollar index hovered near a three-week low as Treasury yields fell sharply, offering limited relief to the rupiah. Locally, fiscal concerns lingered amid persistently high oil prices near USD 100 per barrel, given Indonesia’s status as a net oil importer. Worries also grew that Bank Indonesia may face a difficult trade-off between defending currency stability and supporting near-term growth. Earlier this week, Bank Indonesia kept its key interest rate unchanged for a fourth month but raised the deposit facility rate by 25bps. It also gradually slashed the reserve requirement ratio for commercial banks effective Nov. 1, adding around IDR 190 trillion to the financial system. For the week, the rupiah was on track for a second consecutive weekly decline, down around 0.7% so far, bringing its year-to-date loss to roughly 8%.

【美債週四重挫 美聯儲下週加息預期暴跌】聯邦基金期貨市場目前預計，美聯儲今年已不會再加息的機率升至了24%。與此前火熱CPI資料出爐後的市場預期相比，這堪稱是一次劇烈轉向。與此同時，債券市場也正經歷一場劇烈的重新定價：2年期美債收益率週四重挫18個基點至4.64%。最新定價顯示，針對下週的美聯儲議息會議，市場目前押注加息的機率僅為7%，創下本月新低；12月加息的機率雖仍有76%，但已不再板上釘釘；而1月份的掉期定價也僅體現了27個基點的利率上調幅度——換言之，市場目前認為美聯儲在未來三次會議上，可能只會加息一次。本輪行情的直接導火索，是AI泡沫疑似破裂的“洩氣聲”——面對維繫龐大算力所需的無底洞式資金規模，投資者的心理防線終於失守。Meta在財報與備案中丟擲資本開支需“數千億美元”的鉅額指引，直接嚇崩了市場情緒，其股價盤中應聲重挫近7%；而管理層試圖透過增設無表決權新股來配資的做法，其帶來的股權稀釋效應也招致了投資者的強烈牴觸。

### 2026-10-23 14:35:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Danish Retail Sales Growth Picks Up in September — Retail sales in Denmark rose 4.4% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 3.9% increase in the previous month. Sales grew at a faster pace for consumer electronics (21.7% vs 20.1% in August), supermarkets, department stores, and specialized grocery stores (1.5% vs 1.3%), household equipment, furniture, and building materials (8.9% vs 6.9%), clothing, footwear, and jewelry (9.6% vs 7.9%), and second-hand goods (26.6% vs 19.3%). On the other hand, growth moderated for cultural and leisure products (10.8% vs 13.3%) and pharmaceuticals and personal care products (4.3% vs 5.8%). Moreover, sales declined at a faster pace at liquid fuel filling stations (-17.0% vs -16.8%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail sales increased 0.4%, rebounding from a 0.3% fall in August.

### 2026-10-23 14:10:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Danish Business Morale Hits 18-Month High — Denmark’s manufacturing confidence indicator climbed to 102.7 in October 2026 from 100.5 in the previous month. The latest result moved further above the long-term average of 100 and marked the highest reading since April 2025. Production expectations strengthened to 22 from 15, while employment expectations eased to 2 from 5. On the broader front, the business confidence indicator, covering industry, construction, services and retail, fell to 104.5 from 107.1. Construction sentiment weakened, with its gauge declining to 100.6 from 101.5, while the services measure dropped to 104.9 from 107.3 as revenue expectations fell to 8 from 12 and employment expectations slipped to 1 from 4. Retail optimism recorded the sharpest decline, with its indicator plunging to 103.7 from 114.8. Although revenue expectations remained relatively strong at 29, retail employment expectations turned negative, falling to -3 from 9.

### 2026-10-23 14:08:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Retail Sales MoM #Importance 2

UK Retail Sales Fall More Than Expected — UK retail sales volumes fell 0.4% month-on-month in September 2026, more than the expected 0.1% decline, following an upwardly revised 0.7% rise in August. The drop reflected weaker demand for autumn and winter clothing ranges amid unseasonably warm weather, alongside lower footfall and consumer cutbacks that weighed on non-food retailers. Online clothing sales weakened, while supermarket sales declined after increasing in July and August. Non-food store sales volumes dropped 0.4%, with clothing and footwear, sports equipment and household goods retailers recording declines. In contrast, automotive fuel sales rose despite higher prices. Over the third quarter, retail sales volumes increased 0.7% from the previous quarter and 1.5% year-on-year, supported by higher fuel and food store sales, warm weather, and stronger sales of alcohol and beverages linked to promotions and the World Cup. Annually, retail sales volumes rose 0.8% in September.

【受颱風影響 福建沿海56條客渡運航線停航 轉移13.41萬人】今天，受颱風“薔琵”影響，福建沿海出現8級及以上大風。根據福建省氣象臺預報，今天，閩東、閩中、閩南漁場和釣魚島海域最大風力9—11級、陣風11—13級；臺灣淺灘、閩外漁場及中北部沿海海區最大風力8—10級、陣風10—12級。福建省防汛抗旱指揮部維持防颱風三級應急響應。為了確保安全，截至23日11時，福建沿海共有56條客渡運航線、130艘客渡船停航，其中，4條兩岸“小三通”客運航線全部停航。111個水上施工作業專案全部停工。福建海事局已調派部署專業大型救助船、海巡船艇、應急拖輪等海上應急力量靠前值守，隨時做好應急搶險準備。目前，福建全省海上漁船和沿海養殖漁排上的人員已全部撤離，全省累計轉移海上、陸上危險區域群眾13.41萬人。

### 2026-10-23 13:51:27  #國際

【日本能源白皮書：中東局勢暴露日本能源體系弱點】日本政府23日釋出的2025財年（2025年4月至2026年3月）能源白皮書指出，隨著中東局勢惡化，日本能源供需結構的脆弱性再次凸顯。 白皮書指出，長期以來，日本原油進口超九成依賴中東。今年2月底美國和以色列對伊朗發動軍事打擊後，作為全球能源運輸重要通道的霍爾木茲海峽通航受阻，原油及石油產品供應面臨不確定性，石腦油等部分石油相關產品的流通因此出現阻滯。 為保障國內能源供應，日本政府自3月以來採取了釋放石油儲備、加大從中東以外地區替代採購力度等措施。白皮書認為，這些措施幫助日本確保了原油供應。但與此同時，部分石油相關產品出現了因市場對供應前景擔憂而引發的囤積庫存情況，導致流通不暢和供應分佈不均。 白皮書認為，日本在上世紀70年代石油危機以後數十年間建立的能源危機應對機制，已難以應對新的能源供應風險。 日本政府通常在每年6月前後釋出能源白皮書，今年為納入中東局勢變化情況及相關應對措施，推遲至10月釋出。(新華社)

NZX 50 Ends Slightly Lower, Posts Weekly Loss — The NZX 50 shed 13 points, or 0.1%, to close at 13,734 on Friday, extending losses from the previous session and tracking a fall on Wall Street overnight as a tech-sector selloff deepened. Caution prevailed ahead of the RBNZ’s monetary policy decision next week, as well as the Fed’s interest rate decision. Although annual inflation slowed to 3.7% in Q3, caution persisted amid expectations that inflation would rise again following a temporary dip in fuel prices. Inflation also remained above the RBNZ’s 1–3% target range. Rising oil prices further pressured sentiment, raising concerns over inflation expectations and interest rate hikes. The materials, technology, and energy sectors mainly weighed on the index, with the biggest losses recorded by Fletcher Building (-4.8%), Summerset Group (-3.2%), Henderson Far East Income (-2.7%), EBOS Group (-1.9%), Contact Energy (-1.1%), and Infratil (-1.0%). For the week, the index edged down 0.1% after being almost flat in the previous week.

### 2026-10-23 13:28:35  #市場

【華創證券首席經濟學家張瑜：產業創新的載體正在重構A股的盈利結構與估值錨】華創證券首席經濟學家張瑜表示，資本市場信心的重建，長期需要落腳到企業盈利與產業創新上。產業創新對A股長期價值的賦能，本質上是把“成長性”切實轉化為“盈利性”，讓指數的上漲有利潤的支撐、有產業的根基。中游裝備製造業甚至更廣義的新經濟，正是這條邏輯下最清晰的載體。張瑜表示，產業創新的載體，即中游裝備製造業和更廣義的新經濟，正在重構A股的盈利結構與估值錨。產業競爭力和附加值的提升帶來利潤率和ROE的提升，進而帶來新經濟的市值佔比與指數中樞的長期抬升。制度完善為市場提供環境，產業創新為市場提供盈利，二者共振，A股長期價值的根基就會越扎越牢。

### 2026-10-23 13:21:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Singapore #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Singapore Inflation Rate Edges Lower — Singapore’s annual inflation rate edged down to 2.2% in September 2026 from market expectations and August's more than two-year low of 2.3%. Price growth notably moderated for transport (7.4% vs 8.1% in August), clothing and footwear (2.1% vs 3.1%), and household durables and services (0.2% vs 1.2%). In contrast, inflation slightly picked up for food (2.4% vs 2.3%) and housing and utilities (1.4% vs 1.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.2%, slowing from 0.6% in August. Singapore’s core inflation eased to 2% in September from 2.2% in August, undershooting market expectations of 2.3% as the import-reliant economy weathered renewed global oil price pressures. The slowdown supported the Monetary Authority of Singapore’s recent decision to keep monetary policy unchanged. The central bank also lowered its core inflation forecast for this year to 1.5%-2%, from a previous projection of up to 2.5%, while maintaining its outlook for headline inflation at around 2%.

【西藏巴青7.0級地震 交通運輸部啟動公路交通突發事件三級應急響應】10月22日22時33分許，西藏那曲市巴青縣發生7.0級地震。交通運輸部高度重視，堅決貫徹落實習近平總書記重要指示精神和李強總理批示要求，迅速部署地震應對工作，於23日9時啟動公路交通突發事件三級應急響應，派出工作組趕赴現場，指導西藏自治區交通運輸部門全力做好搶險救援和公路保通保暢等工作。商財政部按照快速核撥機制，安排公路應急搶通資金予以支援。西藏自治區交通運輸廳在地震發生後即啟動二級應急響應，組織當地公路部門安排178人、機械裝置39臺，針對受影響的G317、G349、G345、G558、G562，S301、S205、S208、S209、S302、S506、S303等路段迅速開展排查，目前，已排查公路2377公里、橋樑295座、涵洞635道。經排查，G345、S301部分路段不同程度出現災損，震區公路已採取交通管制措施，救援車輛可保障通行，社會車輛暫不允許通行。

### 2026-10-23 13:10:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

India Manufacturing Growth Slightly Eases — India's HSBC Manufacturing PMI edged down to 54.9 in October 2026 from 55.1 in the previous month, indicating continued growth in factory activity, albeit at a slower pace. Manufacturing output and new orders continued to expand at broadly unchanged rates, while new export orders increased at their fastest pace since June 2025. Manufacturers increased their stocks of purchases at the fastest rate in eight months, while finished goods inventories rose at the sharpest pace on record as firms responded to stronger new orders. However, growth in purchasing activity eased to its slowest pace since the current expansion began in July 2021. Manufacturing employment increased only slightly as firms had sufficient staffing capacity to handle workloads. On prices, manufacturing input cost inflation eased, while selling price inflation slowed to its weakest pace in almost four years.

### 2026-10-23 13:03:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Composite PMI #Importance 2

India Private Sector Growth Picks Up in October — India’s HSBC Composite PMI rose to 56.7 in October 2026 from a final 55.9 in the prior month, flash data showed. It was the fastest growth in private-sector activity since June, with the services sector accelerating while factory output stayed resilient. New orders quickened, and foreign sales increased the most in six months, reflecting robust demand from the U.S., the UK, the Middle East and Bangladesh. However, employment rose at its slowest pace this year. Backlogs of work fell for the third time in four months, with the pace of depletion matching August 2021’s 6-1/2-year high and ranking among the sharpest since the global financial crisis. Price pressures continued to ease, as input cost inflation slowed for a seventh straight month to its lowest in near a year, while output price inflation hit its lowest since March 2025. Looking ahead, confidence reached its highest since May on expectations of stronger market conditions, although it remained below the average for 2026 so far.

### 2026-10-23 12:42:53  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Sensex Falls for Fourth Straight Session — India’s BSE Sensex edged down about 0.4% to 72,970 on Friday, extending losses for a fourth consecutive session as surging crude oil prices and escalating geopolitical tensions continued to weigh on sentiment. Brent crude climbed to $113.50 a barrel amid heightened Middle East tensions and concerns over potential supply disruptions following Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia. Elevated oil prices pose risks to India’s import bill, inflation outlook, and corporate margins, keeping investors wary. Meanwhile, gains in technology stocks, following an overseas rebound after a sell-off in AI-linked shares, provided limited support. Most sectors opened in negative territory, with technology the only sector posting gains. Shares of Infosys, TCS, HCL Tech, and Wipro rose between 1.6% and 3.1%. Among notable laggards included State Bank of India (-0.6%), Cupid (-4.2%), Tech Mahindra (-0.9%), and BSE (-1.2%). For the week to date, the benchmark is on track for a 2.4% decline.

### 2026-10-23 12:32:28  #公司 #焦點

【中際旭創：預計1.6T、800G等高階光模組需求將保持快速增長】中際旭創午間釋出關於公司近期生產經營情況的公告，公司目前生產經營一切正常，供應鏈穩定，產能利用率充足，客戶訂單需求強勁。2017 年-2025 年，公司營業收入從23.57億元增長至382.40億元，年均複合增長率為41.67%；歸屬於上市公司股東的淨利潤從1.62億元增長至107.97億元，年均複合增長率69.03%。2026年上半年營業收入417.78億元，同比增長182.49%；歸屬於上市公司股東的淨利潤136.51億元，同比增長241.70%。在未來的經營發展中，公司將利用現有優勢資源持續拓展光互連解決方案，緊抓行業快速發展的契機，持續強化技術創新、快速量產、規模優勢、質量保障等核心競爭力，進一步最佳化和拓寬公司高階產品系列，保持經營業績穩健前行。

下游需求保持高景氣，公司在手訂單飽滿。海外市場方面，主要客戶訂單已下至2027年，部分重點客戶已給出2028年需求的明確指引，預計1.6T、800G等高階光模組需求將保持快速增長，NPO、XPO等下一代光互連產品需求亦有望逐步釋放。國內市場方面，AI算力基礎設施建設快速推進，400G、800G等產品需求亦有望進一步增長。

光互連技術持續迭代，行業技術壁壘有望進一步提升。公司將圍繞scale-out、scale-up、scale-across 和 scale-in 等全場景網路架構，積極開發下一代光互連解決方案，進一步加大對1.6T、3.2T及更高速率光模組的研發投入，同時積極開發NPO、XPO等下一代產品，目前已在部分產品上與重點客戶開展合作研發與送樣。依託公司在矽光、TFLN、相干等核心技術領域多年的沉澱和先發優勢，公司將持續鞏固技術領先地位，加快推進下一代產品的研發與產業化程序。

### 2026-10-23 12:30:06  #其他

【央視曝光養生鈦製品背後貓膩 你買的鈦製品可能是鍍鈦】從保溫杯、湯鍋到餐盤餐具，鈦制生活用品熱度持續走高。這些商品往往宣稱具有抑菌、保鮮、活化食材等作用，各類養生概念鋪天蓋地。但是同樣打著“鈦”標籤的產品，從一兩百元到一兩千元，價格卻相差不少，普通消費者很難分辨材質真偽與宣傳虛實。 南京工業大學材料科學與工程學院教授常輝介紹，價格便宜的鈦製品很可能是鍍鈦、覆鈦產品，通常是不鏽鋼或鋁，而非鈦合金或純鈦，與整體純鈦有本質區別。“塗層因為跟基體的結合強度可能不太一致，或出現脫落，一旦脫落之後，最大的風險就是在水杯裡面或者鍋具裡面會析出一些重金屬，對人體的健康造成一定危害。” 在宣傳中，還有的商家稱鈦杯可以改變水質酸鹼度、產生富氫水。專家表示，這些從科學上來講應該都是噱頭，沒有什麼科學依據。 專家表示，把鍍鈦產品冒充純鈦售賣、誇大養生功效，涉嫌虛假宣傳，嚴重的構成消費欺詐。直播間、商品頁宣傳不一致，電商平臺也要負相應的責任。（央視財經）

【滙豐上線首枚穩定幣RedCoin 年內可用於認購代幣化投資產品】從滙豐獲悉，滙豐今日在PayMe by HSBC上線首枚穩定幣 “滙豐RedCoin”（簡稱 RCHKD）。上線後的數天內，超過330萬名PayMe使用者可在應用程式內開通穩定幣錢包，支援兩類支付場景：個人對個人轉賬，使用者可透過PayMe向親友即時劃轉穩定幣；個人對商戶支付，可直接在超10萬家合作商戶消費結算。滙豐還計劃在今年晚些時候，在滙豐香港流動理財應用程式(HSBCHK Mobile Banking App)加入穩定幣錢包功能，客戶後續可使用匯豐 RedCoin認購代幣化投資產品，其中包含滙豐港元貨幣市場基金新股份類別。這也是香港金管局批出的首批兩張穩定幣牌照對應的產品之一，另一張牌照由渣打牽頭設立的合資公司碇點金融持有，該機構發行的港元穩定幣HKDAP已於今年8月率先推出。

### 2026-10-23 12:18:48

【震盪市中壓艙石：紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）半日成交4.78億元居同類首位 近5年回報50.17％】10月23日，市場早間震盪調整，創業板指跌超2％，滬指跌1.20％。在此背景下，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）逆勢上漲0.08％，報1.203元，換手率1.57％，半日成交額4.78億元，居同類標的ETF首位。中金公司表示，回顧過去幾年投資結構，從紅利、科技、科技成長啞鈴，自下而上的個股機會成為影響因素，即銀行股更容易在反直覺的承壓環境下跑贏。考慮當前高股息依然是銀行板塊上漲的核心邏輯，增量資金需求一方面來自險資的中長期配置需求，另一方面來自年底降低組合波動性的交易性資金需求，故銀行個股層面，建議關注幾條主線：1）業績與股息兼具的頭部區域行，包括江蘇、南京、渝農等；2）綜合收益波動率較低、分紅預期強的國有大行；3）股息邏輯可能外溢的優質頭部股份行，例如招行等。博時基金認為，後市A股整體維持結構性行情的機率較大，指數層面的方向性突破條件尚不充分。配置上宜以均衡框架應對震盪：紅利資產作為底倉對沖波動，科技方向側重前期調整較充分、三季報盈利兌現能力可驗證的細分領域，結合業績視窗擇機而非追漲。資源品在供給約束與地緣溢價下仍具備階段性對沖價值，但需注意油價衝高對中下游成本的傳導。整體以結構選擇而非倉位博弈應對不確定性。截止2026年10月22日，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）近5年回報50.17％，在1244只產品中排在41位。投資者可將該ETF作為底倉配置，無股票賬戶的投資者也可透過其場外聯接基金進行配置（A類：007466；C類：007467；I類：022678；Y類：022951）。

### 2026-10-23 12:16:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Palm Oil Set for 3rd Straight Weekly Gain — Malaysian palm oil futures extended gains, holding above MYR 4,800 per tonne amid strength in rival edible oils and a tightening supply outlook linked to severe El Niño conditions and Indonesia's upcoming B50 biodiesel mandate. Robust exports also supported prices, with cargo surveyors reporting a 58.5%–78.4% surge in shipments during Oct. 1–20 from the same period in Sept. Festival-related buying from India, the world's largest palm oil importer, has been a key demand driver. The Malaysian Palm Oil Council expects prices to stay above MYR 4,600 in Nov., noting that India and China may increase their reliance on palm oil amid anticipated supply constraints in sunflower and soybean oils. Elevated energy prices further supported prices by strengthening biodiesel demand prospects. However, gains were tempered by softer food-sector demand amid lingering inflation pressures. For the week, futures were on track for a third consecutive weekly rise, up nearly 1% so far.

### 2026-10-23 12:12:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #Leading Economic Index #Importance 1

Malaysia Leading Index Rises 0.2% in August — Malaysia's leading economic index increased 0.2% month-on-month in August 2026, following an upwardly revised 0.6% rise in July. The slowdown reflected declines in real imports of other basic precious and non-ferrous metals (-0.6% vs 0.1% in July), the number of housing units approved (-0.3% vs 0.04%), and real imports of semiconductors (-0.7% vs 0.4%). These declines were partly offset by faster growth in the expected sales value of manufacturing (0.6% vs. 0.1%) and steady growth in real money supply M1 (at 0.1%). On an annual basis, the leading index climbed 2.2%, accelerating from an upwardly revised 1.5% in July. Meanwhile, the coincident index, a gauge of current economic conditions, inched down 0.1% month-on-month, matching the pace recorded in July.

Rupee Rally Stalls on Rising Oil Prices, Dollar Demand — The Indian rupee hovered around 94.8 per dollar, easing slightly from multi-week highs as rising oil prices and a global flight to safety weighed on the currency, while persistent dollar demand threatened to limit further gains. Brent crude climbed to nearly USD 115 a barrel amid concerns over supply disruptions following Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia, raising India’s import costs and potentially spurring further hedging by importers. Meanwhile, the rupee’s eight-session rally ended on Thursday as the currency reversed from a more than six-week high near 94.30. Although the RBI’s recent foreign-exchange measures have curbed dollar demand and provided some relief, they have not addressed underlying pressures from elevated oil prices and capital outflows.

【網傳家電國補正在退場是假的】在社交平臺上，多個博主此前發帖說“家電國補即將停止”。配合著“最後機會”“額度沒了”的話術，讓不少正打算趁“雙十一”換家電的人，一下子不知道該不該等。事實上，今年家電以舊換新補貼政策仍在實施。國家發展改革委9月30日公佈，今年第四批625億元消費品以舊換新資金已下達。 家電經銷商和網路博主很可能是利用消費者的焦慮心理，暗示“等雙十一再下單就趕不上國補”，以此催促消費者提前下單。 實際上，國補的發放工作有節奏和計劃，並不像網路博主所說的一樣緊張。商務部等5部門辦公廳（室）《關於做好2026年家電以舊換新、數碼和智慧產品購新補貼工作的通知》中明確要求：各地要科學制定補貼資金使用及兌付計劃，按月均衡使用，並做好月內每週和跨月、跨季度之間的平穩銜接。記者致電了安徽、重慶、江西等地的商務主管部門，對方均表示國補額度充足，不存在已經用完的情況。（央視財經）

### 2026-10-23 11:27:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Corn Holds Near Two-Month Low — Corn futures held around $4.6 per bushel, near their lowest level in more than two months as weak export shipments and US harvest pressure outweighed stronger new-crop sales. USDA data showed US corn export sales for the 2026/27 marketing year rose 36% week over week to 60.1 million bushels in the week ended October 15, exceeding analysts' estimates and signaling solid demand for future deliveries. However, export inspections plunged nearly 49% to 672,475 metric tons, marking the lowest weekly total since October 2024 and highlighting a slowdown in actual shipments. The US harvest reached 44% completion as of October 18, up from 33% a week earlier, with dry weather across much of the Midwest expected to help fieldwork advance. The USDA projects record US production of 16.03 billion bushels and ending stocks of 1.849 billion bushels for 2026/27, reinforcing concerns over ample availability. These supply pressures continue to limit the market's recovery despite stronger export bookings.

Soybeans Rebound on Strong Export Demand — Soybean futures rose toward $12.6 per bushel, rebounding from a two-month low reached on October 21, as strong export demand led by China helped end an eight-session losing streak. The USDA reported 2.84 million metric tons of net US soybean export sales for the 2026/27 marketing year in the week ended October 15, sharply above 574,900 tons a week earlier. China accounted for 2.18 million tons, while export shipments climbed 75% week over week to 2.02 million tons, up 88% from the four-week average. A separate USDA announcement also confirmed a sale of 202,000 tons to China. Still, futures remained on track for a second consecutive weekly loss as the advancing US harvest and expectations for a large crop continued to weigh on prices. The USDA projects US soybean production at 4.56 billion bushels for the 2026/27 marketing year. However, analysts suggested harvest-related pressure was easing as fieldwork neared completion.

Indonesia Money Supply Growth Edges Up — Indonesia’s broad money supply (M2) rose 8.3% year-on-year to a record IDR 10,587.9 trillion in September 2026, edging up from 8.2% in the previous month. The increase was mainly driven by narrow money supply (M1), which grew 9%, while quasi-money growth accelerated to 7.3%. By component, net claims on the central government (Pempus) rebounded 24.4% after declining 7% in August, while net foreign assets returned to growth of 1% following a 6.6% contraction. However, credit growth eased to 12.7% in September from 13.3% in the previous month.

### 2026-10-23 11:07:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Fall Back to 2-Month Low — US natural gas prices fell back to $2.76 per MMBtu on Friday, their lowest level in two months, as mild weather forecasts weighed on expected heating demand. Above-average temperatures are expected across the Midwest and East through November 5, according to Commodity Weather Group, likely limiting gas consumption. This outweighed support from a smaller-than-usual storage build, strong LNG feedgas demand, and lower production. EIA data showed that energy companies added 77 bcf of natural gas to storage in the week ended October 16, below the 86 bcf injected during the same week last year and the five-year average of 87 bcf. Meanwhile, average gas flows to the nine major US LNG export facilities rose to 18.2 bcfd in October, up from 17.9 bcfd in September. On the supply side, natural gas production across the Lower 48 states averaged 111.4 bcfd this month, down from record highs of 113.3 bcfd in August and September.

Indonesian Stocks Rise for 4th Session, Set for Solid Weekly Gain — Indonesia’s IDX Composite rose 40 points, or 0.7%, to 5,422 in early trading on Friday, up for the fourth session, amid gains in energy, cyclical, industrial, and infrastructure stocks. Sentiment was supported by exchange reforms that will exclude stocks classified as having High Shareholding Concentration from composite index calculations starting Nov. 2. The move follows MSCI warnings that Indonesian equities risk being downgraded from emerging to standalone market status. Meanwhile, several listed companies, including major banks, have launched share buyback programs to restore investor confidence and support sliding valuations. However, gains were capped by fiscal concerns as oil prices hovered around USD 100 per barrel, while accelerating food prices added to inflationary pressures. Bukit Asam rose 3.5%, along with Astra Intl. (2.9%), and XLSmart Telecom (2.5%). For the week, the index was on track to rise around 4%, partially recovering from a nearly 15% plunge in the prior week.

Philippines 10-Year Yield Hits 8-Year High — The Philippines’ 10-year government bond yield climbed to 8.10% in late October, its highest level since October 2018, following the Bangko Sentral ng Pilipinas’ (BSP) larger-than-expected rate hike. The central bank raised its key policy rate by 50 bps to 5.50%, exceeding expectations for a 25 bps increase and marking its fourth consecutive hike this year. The decision came as annual inflation accelerated to 7.2% in September from 6.1% in August, overshooting forecasts of 6.6% and matching April’s three-year high. Higher global oil prices amid the ongoing Middle East tensions and potential El Niño-related supply disruptions threaten to sustain inflationary pressures, while peso weakness raises the cost of imported goods. Meanwhile, subdued demand at recent government bond auctions added to upward pressure on yields, as investors demanded higher returns to compensate for persistent inflation risks.

European Gas Prices Extend Rally — European natural gas prices extended their rally, rising to €91/MWh on Friday to hit a fresh multi-year high as ongoing disruptions to Persian Gulf supplies kept the global LNG market tight ahead of winter. Reports of US preparations for a possible new attack on Iran after the US midterm elections raised the risk of sustained supply disruptions. LNG shipments from the Middle East remained around 80% below year-ago levels, setting the stage for more intense competition between Europe and Asia, the world’s two largest importing regions, for available cargoes. European gas storage stood at 74.62% of capacity as of October 22, the lowest seasonal level since records began in 2009, leaving the region with a limited buffer ahead of winter and increasing its vulnerability to further supply disruptions and stronger heating demand. European gas prices have climbed 4% this week, their fourth consecutive weekly gain.

China Stocks Slide to Over 2-Month Low — The Shanghai Composite lost 1.5% to 3,602 on Friday, while the Shenzhen Component slid 2.1% to 11,118, with both indexes hitting their lowest levels in more than two months as steep losses in semiconductor and technology stocks weighed on the market. The selloff mirrored broader declines across Asian markets following overnight losses on Wall Street, as mounting concerns over the sustainability and potential returns of artificial intelligence investments pressured technology stocks. Domestically, MiniMax Group Inc. and Z.AI Co. have lost a combined $150 billion in market value from their respective peaks amid intensifying competition and the prospect of rival listings. Potential IPOs by DeepSeek and Moonshot AI could further divert investor capital from MiniMax and Zhipu, two of China’s few listed pure-play AI firms. Notable laggards included Cambricon Technologies (-3.7%), SMIC (-2.9%), Zhongji Innolight (-3.6%), Eoptolink Technology (-6.1%) and NAURA Technology (-1.9%).

### 2026-10-23 10:06:02  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #CSI300 #Impact bullish #SSE Composite #CNH

【Guan Tao: Take a rational view of overseas market turbulence spillovers】Guan Tao, chief economist at Huafu Securities, said recent domestic market weakness reflects sentiment volatility after rising geopolitical tensions and widening divergence in overseas tech stocks, but key market drivers remain intact. He listed four market-positive points: China’s long-term favorable economic fundamentals are unchanged; the certainty of stronger, more effective policy support remains; innovation momentum is gradually releasing; and valuation advantages in Chinese assets remain to be exploited. Under dual forces of market demand and policy guidance, China’s growth is shifting toward higher quality and structural upgrade. Investors should treat recent volatility rationally, focus on earnings-recovery stories, and remain cautiously optimistic on medium-to-long-term market prospects. Guan added that deepening capital-market financing and investment reform is two-way: authorities must strengthen institutional support for long-term capital and investors must adopt long-term, value-driven, rational investment habits. The current episode is a litmus test for the market’s ability to move away from momentum trading toward greater maturity.

【六部門出臺意見 推進老舊街區廠區改造提升】從住房城鄉建設部獲悉，住房城鄉建設部、工業和資訊化部等六部門近日聯合印發《關於推進老舊街區廠區改造提升的指導意見》，明確了各類老舊街區廠區的改造提升重點。按照要求，各地要結合城市體檢結果，將房屋和市政基礎設施老化、公共服務和公共空間短缺、功能產業和發展活力有待提升的街區廠區作為改造提升物件，統籌確定改造提升範圍和實施時序。同時，制定老舊街區廠區改造提升專案實施方案，將城市設計要求納入專案實施方案，引導街區廠區高質量改造提升。其中，重點要做好五項工作：推動老舊廠區功能轉換推動老舊商業街區業態升級推動老舊生活街區品質提升推動老舊交通樞紐及周邊街區融合發展加強歷史文化街區鎮區保護利用為做好相關工作，各地要健全全生命週期實施機制，發揮政府引導作用，加大政策支援力度。同時，建立可持續的投融資模式，支援各類企業參與老舊街區廠區改造提升，規範市場化運作流程等。

### 2026-10-23 10:05:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Platinum Sinks to 11-Month Low — Platinum futures dropped below $1,560 an ounce, hitting their lowest level since November 2025, as a liquidity-driven selloff swept across precious metals amid a broader stock market rout. Growing concerns over the sustainability of AI investment triggered a sharp decline in US stocks and a rally in Treasuries, prompting investors to liquidate gold and other liquid assets to meet margin calls and cover losses elsewhere. Non-yielding metals also remained under pressure from rising oil prices, which heightened inflation concerns and strengthened expectations that interest rates could stay elevated for longer. Platinum also faces fundamental headwinds, with the World Platinum Investment Council forecasting a 265,000-ounce market surplus in 2026, as weaker investment demand and softer jewelry consumption weigh on the outlook. Automotive demand is also expected to decline 4%, partly offset by a projected 5% increase in industrial demand.

【管濤：理性看待海外資本市場動盪對國內的溢位影響】受地緣衝突緊張局勢升級、海外科技股行情演進分歧加大的影響，近期國內資本市場調整加劇，反映了市場情緒的波動。華福證券首席經濟學家管濤在接受採訪時表示，從以下方面看，中國資產的配置價值更加凸顯，資本市場走勢與之存在一定錯位。一是經濟長期向好的基本面沒有變；二是政策加力提效的確定性沒有變；三是創新動能正在逐步釋放；四是市場估值優勢有待挖掘。管濤表示，在市場需求和政策引導的雙重驅動下，中國經濟向新向優、結構升級趨勢明確，投資者應理性看待市場震盪，把握業績修復主線，對市場中長期走勢保持審慎樂觀。同時，深化資本市場投融資綜合改革是“雙向奔赴”，既要從制度上不斷完善長錢長投的資本市場生態環境，也要投資者不斷強化長期投資、價值投資、理性投資的投資理念。當前正是前述投資理念的“試金石”，是中國資本市場擺脫“追漲殺跌”、走向更加成熟的又一次大考。（證券時報）

### 2026-10-23 10:01:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Currency #Importance 1

New Zealand Dollar Hovers Near 1-Month High — The New Zealand dollar edged up to $0.569, trading near a one-month high, as expectations of further monetary tightening by the Reserve Bank supported the currency. Although inflation eased slightly more than expected in the third quarter, analysts expect price pressures to pick up again, potentially pushing inflation well above 4% in the fourth quarter and keeping it outside the RBNZ’s 1-3% target range for longer than previously anticipated. This outlook was reinforced by rising energy prices amid heightened tensions in the Middle East. Markets are almost certain that the central bank will deliver a third rate hike next week, bringing the OCR to 3.0%. Beyond that, some economists project the OCR to climb to 3.75% by the first quarter of next year or peak at 4% in the second half of 2027. The kiwi has gained 0.7% this week, marking its second consecutive weekly advance.

【中汽協付炳鋒：企業要把關鍵核心技術攻關擺在首要位置】日前，中國汽車工業協會副會長兼秘書長付炳鋒在2026中國汽車供應鏈大會上稱，在國際複雜多變的地緣政治因素影響下，區域的貿易保護對汽車產業鏈、供應鏈穩定造成了巨大的衝擊。“站在‘十五五’開局新起點，‘保量提質’成為發展的主旋律。”付炳鋒稱。付炳鋒建議，行業、企業要把關鍵核心技術攻關擺在首要位置，聚焦車規級晶片、車載作業系統、固態電池、新型電子電氣架構、基礎工業軟體等短板持續攻堅，築牢產業鏈供應鏈安全底座。同時，要深化電動化、智慧化、綠色化融合發展，強化整零協同與跨界融合，推動整車企業與內、外資核心零部件供應商構建協同共創體系，以生態化協作提升產業鏈整體韌性與創新效率。
